“Is there a soulmate for me after the marriage ended?”

?hook=cards-after-marriage

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 55-64 BINARY rung. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NEVER PRESUME HOW THE MARRIAGE ENDED. 'The marriage ended' covers divorce AND death, and the theme pull (sm-after-marriage, 2026-08-19) returned both — 21 buyers, most divorced, and a real bereaved slice ('lost many loved ones recently, starting over at this age'). So: no 'your divorce', no 'when he left', no 'since he passed', no 'your ex'. The read holds for a widow and for a woman who walked out, or it is wrong for half the traffic. 🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP, for the same reason. 🔴 NO RULING ON THE MAN and NO BLAME ON HER. The same pull is full of abuse, fraud and financial ruin — 'he got very abusive towards me in the end', 'extorted me 8 months with divorcing papers', 'left me with no finances and no home'. Nothing about what she should have done, what she attracted, or what the marriage taught her about herself. ⚠ Meta-sensitive material sits right under this headline: financial hardship and health both surface in the pull. The read goes nowhere near either. 🔴 NO DURATION and NO DATE (see cards-keeps-waiting). The hopeful YES is allowed and is the answer she came for — the directional loosening of 2026-08-19 covers it — but it may never be attached to a time, a place, or a person. The finding: she has been counting to one. One marriage, one chance, and the sum stops there.

“Am I still allowed to want a soulmate?”

?hook=cards-allowed-to-want

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 65+ WHY rung. ⭐ The operator's own line, and the only one in the batch that offers PERMISSION rather than a prediction. Face-down only (return-mhf). The word doing the work is ALLOWED. She is not asking for a forecast, so this is the one lander in the batch whose cut 3 must NOT answer 'yes, they're coming' — answering the wrong question here throws away the only thing that makes the headline different. The theme pull (sm-allowed-to-want, 2026-08-19) also names who took the permission away: 'Friends tell me I'm too old to want a man, but I've been widowed for six years and would like a good man', 'I sometimes feel silly thinking I will have another love at my age', 'At my age, I suppose I should just be content', 'Older mother and adult children with too much to say ...especially at my age .65'. 🔴🔴 NO PREDICTION, NO ARRIVAL, NO TIMING. Not because the directional loosening forbids it — it doesn't — but because it answers a question she did not ask, and because the same pull contains an active romance scam and a woman left destitute at 82. A promised arrival is the sentence a scammer's mark can act on. 🔴🔴 NOTHING ABOUT HER LOOKS, HER BODY, OR HER AGE. 'I've gained weight and I'm so much older', 'I have a hard time accepting what age has done to my face and body..I look very old', 'I don't have my youthful look, I feel very unattractive'. A 'you're still beautiful' line is a lie she can check in a mirror, and it is platform-flagged material besides. No number, no face, no body, ever. 🔴🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP. This pull is the most bereaved of the eleven — 'the love of my life died after 38 years together', 'widowed for six years', 'my partner died 10 years ago', 'my 45 year old daughter passed unexpectedly'. Never presume a death, never speak for one, never 'he'd want you to'. 🔴 NEVER NAME WHO WITHHELD THE PERMISSION. Friends, an older mother, adult children are all named in the pull. Naming a real person as the block is banned funnel-wide. Cut 7 asks WHAT taught her to ask, never WHO. 🔴 DO NOT GENDER THE SOULMATE. Men are in this pull ('will I ever meet her', 'I'm still a Virgin at my age') and so is a same-sex partnership of 38 years. No he, no him, no man, anywhere in the read. 🔴 NO HEALTH AND NO MONEY. Hip replacement, PTSD, 'medically I have to avoid stressful situations', destitution and scam loss all sit in the pull. ⚠ Cut 6 DESCRIBES, it does not certify. 'You have every right to want this' is the psychic ruling on her and it is the exact move the framework bans — hand her back her own week instead. The finding: nobody hands this permission out, so nobody could have taken it back. She has been waiting to be told, and the wanting has picked up an apology on the way.

Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'. 🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE FORBIDDEN, so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the absolute — a quiet sidestep reads as evasion rather than as a reading. The finding that replaces the verdict: 'forever' is not a length. It is what the WEIGHT feels like from underneath, and the weight is the real thing to name.

“Will we get back together?”

?hook=cards-back-together

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). The US-FRAMED sibling of `reunion`, and that pairing is the whole experiment — reunion casts him as the agent and her as the one waiting; these cast the relationship as a joint thing with two people in it. 🔴 THE FORBIDDEN VERDICT IS ON THE RELATIONSHIP, NOT ON HIM — harder, not softer. A claim about a man can at least be measured against his behaviour; 'it is over' or 'it is not over' is unfalsifiable to her and lands as a ruling on her life from someone who has never met either of them. 🔴 NO ODDS — cards-still-a-chance asks for a number outright and there is none; no percentage, no 'strong chance', no 'likely'. 🔴 NO DIRECTIVES in either direction: move on, let go, fight for him, reach out first are all out. 🔴 Never hand her the outcome ('it is up to you', 'if you had only') — the us-framing makes that easy and it lands as blame. Finding, and the one that is truest to the us-framing: this question is about a JOINT thing and only one of the two people is in the room. She has been carrying a two-person decision on her own — which is why it will not resolve however long she turns it over.

“Why did I give my best years to someone who wasn't my soulmate?”

?hook=cards-best-years

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 55-64 WHY rung. Face-down only (return-mhf). The words doing the work are GAVE and BEST YEARS — she is the one who spent, and what she spent cannot be got back. The theme pull (sm-best-years, 2026-08-19) is blunt about it: 'I feel like I've wasted my life loving him', 'I've wasted the last 20 years', 'I invested 30 years of my life, loved him with all my heart, I just don't exist to him', 'I feel sad I wasted my best years on him'. 🔴🔴 SHE IS OFTEN STILL IN IT. The headline is past tense and a large share of the pull is present tense — 'married 37 years to a man I no longer have a connection to', 'married for 35 years to a man who doesn't really value me', 'I am married but there is no love there... I can't leave', 'Living 50 years with someone who's only ever taken'. So: no 'now that it's over', no 'now you're free', no 'that chapter is closed', and NOTHING that tells her to leave. The read must hold for a woman going back to that house tonight. 🔴🔴 NO LESSON, NO PURPOSE MADE OF IT. The finding says she still holds what she became in those years — it may NEVER be phrased as the years having happened in order to teach her. 'It was all for a reason' is the loneliness family's banned move (suffering made purposeful) and it is worse here, because the pull is full of abuse, fraud and a man she visited in prison and funded every week. 🔴 NO BLAME AND NO RULING ON HIM. Domestic abuse, financial abuse, cheating and criminality are all in the pull. Nothing about what she allowed, what she attracted, why she stayed, or what kind of man he was. 🔴 NO MEDIUMSHIP — 'Widowed over a year now after almost 40 years with a man' is in the pull. 🔴 NO HEALTH, NO MONEY ADVICE, NO AGE NUMBERS ('I'm 71 and feel I have led a wasted life', 'now with health problems it's hard to make changes'). 🔴 DISTRESS IS IN THIS PULL — 'The only thing that's keeping me alive is a promise God made to me 18 years ago... I'm actually starting to hate life itself.' The read may not deepen the it-was-all-for-nothing reading. Cut 3 refuses the void flat, on every card. ⚠ MUST NOT RESTATE `cards-choosing-wrong`, which runs on the same age band and the same wrong man. That lander answers HOW SHE PICKED; this one answers WHAT THE TIME COST. Keep the mechanism out of it. The finding: the years were not paid out to him. What she built inside them is still on her, and the thing that hurts is the count she keeps running, not the man.

?hook=cards-blocked-retiring

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Money translation: there is no man here, so cut 4 becomes what the block has been DOING, cut 5 becomes the EARNING against the KEEPING, and cut 6 names the near-miss she has lived — the VOC's own sentence. 🔴 NONE of the seven bans below was loosened with the love families; this is the one place where a wrong sentence costs her actual money. 🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. 🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. ✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired. 📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions. **Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.' **The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.' So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go. Tendency: her ability was never the problem, and something stands between the EARNING and the KEEPING. Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 25-44 WHY rung. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE HEADLINE PRESUPPOSES SHE CHOSE, AND THE VOC SAYS SHE OFTEN DID NOT. The theme pull (sm-choosing-wrong, 2026-08-19) came back as fraud and abuse: 'badly hurt by a man I thought loved me but he was a scammer', 'my marriage broke up because of domestic abuse and I'm scared of yet again choosing a wrong man', 'abusive, and divorced him', 'he turned out to be a convicted paedophile'. Telling a woman who was defrauded or assaulted that she keeps choosing wrong blames her for a crime committed against her. So cut 3 REFUSES THE PREMISE, on every card. She did not choose them; she was shown something else — her own words: 'seemed to be one person but changed along the way'. 🔴 NOTHING about a pattern in her, low self-worth, attachment style, healing herself first, attracting what she is, or needing to fix anything before love arrives. 🔴 NO diagnosis of any kind. 🔴 Never name or convict a real man either — the read describes what was SHOWN, never rules on who he is. 🔴 NO DURATION, NO DATE, NO PROMISED ARRIVAL.

These three name an untruth outright, so both answers are live harms: 'he lied to you' is an accusation against a real man on no evidence, and 'he is telling you the truth' is a reassurance the funnel has no standing to give — and on this audience the second is the more dangerous of the two. 🔴 Never blame her judgment: naive, gullible, too trusting, 'should have known' are all out. 🔴 No timeframe. The two Magician decks share one set, verb swapped. 🔴 decode-him's beat 1s are written fresh rather than kept: all four hooks on that deck currently open with the SAME line per card (no guard file catches it — see the duplication note in the checklist). 🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can. 🔴 BEAT 3 CORRECTED 2026-08-19. tests/tarot-real-feelings-copy.test.ts fired — a deliberate tripwire on this hook, because cards-feels is the CONTROL for two comparisons. The first rewrite dropped the old copy's "leans toward" hedge to reach grade 5, which turned a tendency into a flat verdict on his heart ("He does feel it, dear") — exactly what this hook's TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY bans. Plain English did not require it: "You haven't been reading coldness, dear. You've been reading a hold." says the same thing at the same grade, about HER reading rather than his interior. Rule applied: no sentence may take "he" as the subject of a feeling verb stated as fact; negations ("doesn't mean he's cold") and card-tendency forms ("it reads as") are fine.

“Something feels off — is my intuition right?”

?hook=cards-feels-off

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER CONVICT HIM and 🔴 never dismiss what she noticed — both doors are shut. 🔴🔴 AND NEVER NAME CONTENTS: no other woman, no affair, no money, no secret family, no messages from anyone. Naming a content is the single most harmful thing this pair could do, because she will go looking for it. 🔴 No surveillance and no 'confront him'. ⚠️ THIS HOOK SUBMITS HER OWN JUDGEMENT FOR A VERDICT, which is a trap: 'yes, you are right' convicts him through her, and 'no, you are wrong' teaches her to distrust the one instrument she has. So the question is SPLIT rather than answered — the read certifies that she registered something real, and leaves what it means entirely open. (The word 'intuition' is four syllables and cannot appear at grade 5; the echo lands on 'feels', 'off' and 'right' instead.)

“Am I living in her shadow?”

?hook=cards-her-shadow

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened. ⚠️ "her" may be an EX or a woman who has DIED, and the headline does not say. 🔴 NEVER presume which. 🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP BAN (this family runs the decode-him frame, which carries none of it): never that she is at peace, watching, or would want anything. 🔴 NEVER disparage or characterise her, never make her a rival, never rank the two women. And the tightrope: never confirm the comparison as fact, never tell her she is imagining it. VOC (769 V1 concerns, married-man/other-woman theme) settles why that tightrope is real: the other woman is very often NOT a rival — she is dead, ill, dependent, or entirely legitimate. "He lost his wife of 57 years a short while ago." "His wife is in bad health... I certainly would not hurt his wife." "I'm not a marriage breaker." Handing this reader an enemy would be harm dressed as comfort. So the split, exactly as `hidden-intuition` splits its question: certify HER FEELING (she has felt something in the room) and refuse to rule on the woman at all. The finding does the rest — a shadow is not a person, it is what falls when something blocks the light. Which makes bubble 7 the easiest object on the funnel: the thing blocking the light IS the block Act 1 clears.

the Fool, the card of new beginnings

The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened. 🔴🔴 THE SHARPEST LANDER ON THE FUNNEL. It asks a card to comment on a man's CHILDREN — real third parties, possibly minors, who cannot consent to being read. NEVER rank her against them in either direction, never characterise them, never grade him as a father. The test pins BOTH halves of the payout: beat 3 must say plainly that children DO have a first call (`first call`), AND must offer a DEFINED place rather than a higher one (`place of your own` / `where you sit` / `appear on one` / `planned for you`). VOC (192 V1 concerns on the his-children theme): the injury is NOT rank. Not one woman asked to outrank a child. What they typed: "his daughter is getting married in July and I'm not invited... I've been Keith's partner for nine years", "he had a whole life without me", "I feel rejected from the family", "he lets them disrespect me". She is not on the list at all — in either direction. So the read says the true thing first (first call is real), then names the blur that actually hurts: first call was never the same as only call.

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  2. 2You asked why his children come before you. Your hand went to the man who plans.16w
  3. 3So his children do have a first call, dear. I won't dress that up for you.16w
  4. 4And first call was never the only call, dear. Look how much is on that table.16w
  5. 5But nobody has ever set a place there for you. Not one you could point at.16w
  6. 6That's why you're not asking to move up a list, dear. You're asking to appear on one.17w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what stands between you and a place of your own…15w

TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY: 'never state he is lying or that he is honest as fact; affirm HER instrument'. The VOC makes that a safety rule rather than a style rule — 28 real concerns off this exact lander, and a large share of them have never met the man: 'I just want make sure he is not an impostor', 'he says he loves me... I haven't met him afraid he's a scammer', 'I met this guy online. He's from Nigeria... my gut feeling tells me he's not [coming]'. A line that vouches for him could cost one of these women money. So every beat 3 certifies HER NOTICING and names what she was never given — never a verdict in either direction.   4. 4And look how he holds himself. He's very good at how a thing is put.15w
  5. 5But a smooth account isn't proof either way, dear.9w

“How much longer will something keep blocking my money?”

?hook=cards-how-much-longer

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. 📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free. ✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all). Tendency: a block does not run down like a clock — it lasts as long as it is left alone, so the honest answer is about what gets moved, not a number. 🔴 NEVER give a date, a timeframe or a season, even a soft one.

return-mhf she turned a face-down card

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he could touch all four things without moving his feet.16w
  2. 2You asked how much longer. Your hand went to the card of a person who acts.16w
  3. 3So this isn't a wait, dear. It's a thing that has to be moved.14w
  4. 4And every tool he needs is within reach, dear. Nothing is far off.13w
  5. 5But no number from me, dear. A block doesn't run down like a clock.14w
  6. 6That's why last year looked just like this one, dear. Nothing was touched.13w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has not been touched…10w

“How long does a soulmate keep you waiting?”

?hook=cards-keeps-waiting

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 45-54 HOW-LONG rung. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 NO DURATION — the defining ban of this batch, and the reason four of these landers cannot run under any existing frame. Every frame on the funnel bans a DATE. This headline asks for a LENGTH, and 'not much longer', 'a few more months', 'soon', 'not long now' are all lengths rather than dates, so the date ban does not touch them. Banned outright: any span of time, any pace, any 'by then', any season, forward or back. 🔴 THE REFUSAL MUST BE VISIBLE, not a sidestep. Both answers are unavailable here, so a read that quietly changes the subject reads as evasion and she feels conned — same treatment as cards-alone-forever, which declines the length out loud and then gives her something real instead. ⚠ The corpus warning: in her own words 'how long' almost always means waiting on a SPECIFIC man ('how long should I wait for Jack'). This lander must not answer as though a named man exists. 🔴 No verdict on any real person, no promised arrival, no PLACE, no self-blame, no 'he is worth the wait'. The finding that replaces the verdict: the tiredness is not from the length of the wait. It is from holding it open on her own.

Every reason she has been given arrived without a date. So the read refuses the 'why' (his to give) and answers the shape instead: a plan and a habit look identical from outside, and nobody has told her which one she lives in. The absolution is the one she has never been offered — she has been doing the work of two people, living the arrangement AND translating it afterwards.

“Is he losing interest, or just going through something?”

?hook=cards-losing-interest

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT EITHER WAY — 'he is losing interest' convicts him, 'he still loves you' reassures on his behalf, and 'nothing has gone wrong' is the same reassurance in a smaller coat. 🔴 NO CHASE STRATEGY, which is what the whole internet sells against this headline: no giving him space, no pulling back, no matching his energy, no going quiet so he chases. 🔴 Never blame her — nothing about being too much, too eager, moving too fast, or what she should have held back. 🔴 ITS HEADLINE IS AN EITHER-OR AND BOTH HALVES ARE FORBIDDEN ANSWERS. The test requires every card to VISIBLY refuse the binary — and to never resolve it in passing either. The finding: the choice is not hers to make and was never hers to carry, and she has been running both branches at once for weeks.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER NARRATE HIS INTERIOR — the ban list here is the longest on the funnel and it covers both directions: 'he still loves you' promises the love survived, 'he has moved on' issues the burial, and 'he still thinks about you', 'you cross his mind', 'a part of him always will', 'deep down he' are all the softened confession. 🔴 No directive ('time to let go'), 🔴 no pathologising her ('obsessed', 'stuck', 'clinging', 'can't let go'), and 🔴 nothing presuming HOW he went — not that he chose to walk out, not that he died. 🔴 THE FAMILY'S OWN FINDING, pinned, and it belongs to no other hook: the two branches are NOT OPPOSITES. A person can carry something and still walk on. If this ever drifts to 'neither is knowable' it has become cards-moved-on and the comparison is dead — so it may not restate that incumbent's grounds either. ⚠️ Beat 2 could not restate her headline: 'he still loves you' is itself a banned phrase, so the echo had to be rebuilt as 'if the love is there, or if he has moved past it'.

“Am I meant to be alone?”

?hook=cards-meant-alone

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO FATE, IN ANY FORM — 'you are meant to be alone' is the most harmful sentence available on this funnel, and 'it is meant to be', 'the universe has a plan', 'this is preparing you', 'there is a reason for this' are the same sentence in kinder clothes: they make her suffering purposeful and assign it to her. 🔴 NO LIFE SENTENCE and 🔴 NO PROMISE — 'you will always be alone' and 'you will meet someone' are both strangers ruling on the rest of her life. 🔴 No pathologising: nothing about her being negative, bitter, closed off, self-sabotaging, or 'attracting what she puts out'. Finding: it refuses the PREMISE that anything is being assigned at all — not just the answer. Being alone is a circumstance, not a designation, and nobody wrote it down against her name.

“Is my soulmate still coming, or have I already missed him?”

?hook=cards-missed-chance

Soulmate age-band batch (2026-08-19), 45-54 BINARY rung. ⚠ The hook is `cards-missed-chance`, NOT `cards-already-missed` — that name sits one word away from the live `cards-met-already` ('Have I already met my soulmate without realizing it?') and the two would be misread for each other in every report and every wiring diff. Face-down only (return-mhf). The word doing the work is ALREADY: she believes the event has happened, in the past, and that she failed to notice or failed to hold it. The theme pull (sm-already-missed, 2026-08-19) says it almost verbatim — 'Have I missed out on who is or was to be my husband or soulmate', 'I'm afraid I've already missed my soulmate', 'Wondering if I have missed my chance', 'I feel like I already missed my one chance at real love'. 🔴🔴 NEVER REVIVE A SPECIFIC PAST MAN. The same pull returns 'the one that got away' over and over, and when she names him he is very often married — 'By the time I met him 44 years ago he was married... he loved his wife and would never leave her' — or the man who abandoned her. A read that says the one she is thinking of is still coming points her at a real, identifiable, unavailable person she can go and contact. That is `cards-back-together` territory and it is banned outright here: no him, no the one, no he is still there, nothing that can attach to a man already in her memory. 🔴🔴 NO FERTILITY AND NO CHILDREN. 'I've already missed the opportunity to have children. I'm 45 now.' This is the one window in the pull that genuinely did close, it is Meta-prohibited health material, and there is no honest hopeful half. The read does not go near children, a family, or time running on her body. 🔴 NO DATE. The binary form does not ask for a length, so the duration ban of the HOW-LONG landers is not the live risk here — the date ban is, and it stands. 🔴 DISTRESS IS IN THIS PULL — 'every day I'm getting closer to suicidal thoughts'. The banned half of the binary is not merely unkind, it confirms a loss to someone already at the edge. 'You missed him' may never be said in any form, including softened ones. No mediumship. No place. No blame — she arrives already holding it. The finding: a chance is not one event with a door on it. Nothing went past her that she could have caught, so there was never a moment to fail.

“What does my energy say about why money won't stay?”

?hook=cards-money-wont-stay

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. 📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free. ✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all). Tendency: the drawing-in half has always worked and what is at issue is everything after it arrives. 🔴 NEVER name who or what takes it, never call her careless with money, and never advise her on keeping it.

No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. 📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free. ✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all). Tendency: her energy is not the block and never was; affirm what she has noticed while refusing the fault, exactly as hidden-intuition splits its question. 🔴 NEVER agree that she blocks herself, never mindset, vibration, deserving or self-sabotage, and never hand her a practice to fix herself.

Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions. **Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.' **The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.' So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go. Tendency: long-standing rather than recent, and steady effort that never accumulates points at something in the way rather than at a failing in her. 🔴 NEVER give a length of time — the headline asks for one and it may not be supplied. Never tell her she should have saved differently.

“Will I find a new soulmate after loss?”

?hook=cards-new-soulmate

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD. 🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing. 🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves. ⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death. Finding: the fear underneath is NOT whether such a person exists. It is whether loving again would mean replacing him. VOC, verbatim: 'I'm still going through the grief of losing my husband, so I'm not ready to find love again.' A generic 'yes there is hope' is the incumbent lander and answers nobody.

?hook=cards-not-enough

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NEVER SUPPLY HIS REASON — not scared, not overwhelmed, not 'he couldn't cope', not 'he left because', not 'the reason he'. The reason lives in a man who did not give it, and inventing one hands her something false to act on. 🔴 Never a verdict on his person, 🔴 never invent a third person, and 🔴 note that 'it was never about you' and 'he was protecting you' are banned too — they are motive claims wearing reassurance. 🔴 No tactic in either direction: no reaching out, no one last message, no blocking, no no-contact. 🔴 No promise of closure. 🔴 THE COMPARISON IS REFUSED, NEVER SCORED — and that includes the kind direction. 'You were more than enough' feels generous and is still a claim about why he went, which is a claim nobody here can make. So the read declines to weigh her at all, and names the scale itself as the thing that was handed to her.

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO LOCATION, EVER — never place him, never name a setting, never 'someone you already know', never 'crossed paths before'. 🔴 NO PROXIMITY CLAIM: 'closer than you think' is the INCUMBENT cards-soulmate's answer, and if this family repeats it the two landers are the same lander while the dashboard still shows two rows. 🔴 NO STRATEGY — get out there, put yourself out there, stop looking, work on yourself first, 'it will happen when you…' are all directives. 🔴 Never her fault: no walls, no baggage, no standards, no 'too picky'. Finding: an absence is not always CAUSED, so there is no culprit to hand her — and she has been the only suspect in her own case for a long time.

return-mhf she turned a face-down card

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the cup on his table is gold, and it's empty.16w
  2. 2You asked why you've not found your soulmate yet. Your hand went to the maker.15w
  3. 3So there's no wrong turning to find, dear. You didn't miss it.12w
  4. 4And look at the cup. It's empty, and nobody emptied it.11w
  5. 5But you've been hunting for whose fault this is, dear.10w
  6. 6That's why a quiet year feels like a mark against you, dear.12w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been sitting in the empty place…13w

“Why is he always on my mind?”

?hook=cards-on-my-mind

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe. Finding: the word 'always'. She is reporting a FREQUENCY and asking to be told what it means about her. It means she has had nowhere to put it — an unfinished thing takes up room, and room is not the same as feeling.

return-mhf she turned a face-down card

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his workbench is full and there's no space left on it.17w
  2. 2You asked why he's always on your mind. Your hand went to the man whose bench is full.18w
  3. 3So there's no room left, dear. That's what full looks like.11w
  4. 4And there's not one clear space on that wood, dear.10w
  5. 5But an open thing takes up room, dear. It doesn't ask first.12w
  6. 6That's why he's there when you wake, dear. Room isn't the same as love.14w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been taking up all that room…13w

“Is something still blocking my money, or have I run out of time?”

?hook=cards-out-of-time

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. 📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free. ✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all). Tendency: she has NOT run out of time — say it plainly — and a hold is not an ending. 🔴 NEVER promise an arrival, never a date, and never rule on how much time she has.

  4. 4And this card deals in what's here now, dear. It shows me something here.14w

“Why is he pulling away from me?”

?hook=cards-pulling-away

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO VERDICT EITHER WAY — 'he is losing interest' convicts him, 'he still loves you' reassures on his behalf, and 'nothing has gone wrong' is the same reassurance in a smaller coat. 🔴 NO CHASE STRATEGY, which is what the whole internet sells against this headline: no giving him space, no pulling back, no matching his energy, no going quiet so he chases. 🔴 Never blame her — nothing about being too much, too eager, moving too fast, or what she should have held back. Finding: she is being asked to interpret a change nobody explained, and the interpreting is the exhausting part — not the distance itself.

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his right hand is up and his left hangs open and low.18w
  2. 2You asked why he's pulling away from you. Your hand went to the man who acts on purpose.18w
  3. 3So something did change, dear. The card shows the shift plainly.11w
  4. 4And look at his hands, dear. One reaching up, one letting go low.13w
  5. 5But the reason sits with him, dear, and he hasn't said it out loud.14w
  6. 6That's why guessing has taken up your weeks, dear. It's hard work.12w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has opened up between you…11w
Show what she reads today

Card b

the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — he hangs from one ankle and the rest of him is loose.19w

  3. 3So you're halfway, dear. Not ended, and not held either.10w
  4. 4And nobody has cut him down, dear. He just hangs there.11w

  6. 6That's why evenings leave you spent, dear. You've translated all day.11w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been widening this…10w

Card c

the Fool, the card of new beginnings

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the dog is white and small against all that rock.16w
  2. 2You asked why he's pulling away from you. Your hand went to the one already in motion.17w
  3. 3So he is moving, dear. You saw it before anyone said so.12w
  4. 4And he moves off, dear, with bare rock behind him.10w
  5. 5But motion is all the card gives, dear. It gives no reason.12w
  6. 6That's why you noticed first, dear, and had nobody to tell.11w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been pushing this apart…11w

“Is he ever going to be ready for real commitment?”

Every ban in this note still stands. On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up). 🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them. Finding: the GAP between what SHE is ready for and where HE is standing is real information — and her readiness is legitimate rather than impatience. Kept deliberately distinct from cards-twin-ready, which is its measured pair in tests/tarot-twin-flame-copy.test.ts and may share no 6-word run with it.

“Am I ready to love again after losing him?”

?hook=cards-ready-to-love

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴🔴 THE ONLY FAMILY ON THE FUNNEL WHERE THE MAN MAY BE DEAD. 🔴🔴 MEDIUMSHIP is the failure mode that exists nowhere else here: 'he is at peace', 'he is watching over you', 'he would want you to be happy', 'he sent you here'. Every one is the most natural sentence in the world to say to a grieving woman, and every one is contact with the dead — a different product with a different compliance surface. server/lib/universalSafety.ts does NOT catch it. None of these reads speaks for him, reports his state, or grants his blessing. 🔴 NO ARRIVAL PROMISE and no timeframe — this family runs under AFTER_LOSS_TAROT_HOOKS rather than the self-frame 'affirm the hopeful yes with CERTAINTY' clause, because aimed at a bereaved partner that clause promises a replacement. 🔴 NEVER GRADE HER GRIEF: no 'you are ready', no 'you are not ready', no 'it is too soon', no 'you need more time'. 🔴 No grief directives and never rank the two loves. ⚠️ AND NOTHING MAY PRESUME A DEATH EITHER. The VOC shows 'loss' here is often something else entirely — 'My husband walked out after 21 yrs like I didn't matter at all', 'Husband in a nursing home', 'We broke up a month ago'. The reads stay agnostic about what the loss was, which also keeps them safe if it was a death. 🔴 BOTH ANSWERS ARE BANNED — 'you are ready' and 'you are not ready' are each a stranger grading a widow's grief — so every card must VISIBLY DECLINE the headline rather than sidestep it. The finding that replaces the verdict: PERMISSION here is hers, and nobody else gets a say.

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — the wand he holds is short, and he grips it near the middle.19w
  2. 2You asked about loving again after losing him. Your hand went to the man who builds rather than rules.19w
  3. 3So I will not grade you on this, dear. Nobody gets to.12w
  4. 4And look at his hands. They're for making things, not for measuring them.13w
  5. 5But there's no standard here that anyone could hold you to, dear.12w
  6. 6That's why the question keeps coming back, dear. The permission was yours all along.14w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been standing between you and your own say…15w

“Is he the real person, or just a picture?”

?hook=cards-real-person

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped. ⚠️ TRUST IS A SEED FAMILY WITH NO GUARD FILE (`tests/tarot-trust-copy.test.ts` does not exist). Only the generic guards apply, so this is among the least protected copy on the funnel and the bans below come from TAROT_HOOK_TENDENCY rather than from a test. 🔴🔴 REASSURANCE IS THE FAILURE MODE HERE, NOT THE SAFE OPTION. The 2026-07-10 buyer audit caught Evelyn reframing textbook scam markers as a genuine bond. The standing rule: never reassure her the bond is genuine, never vouch for him — and still never state as fact that he is fake, which is its own verdict. 78 real concerns off this lander are overwhelmingly online and unmet: 'I need to know if it is really him….that I am talking to', 'I am speaking with two men on line', 'he always tells me he loves me and misses me so much' followed by 'sometimes he just disappears'. What gets affirmed is HER CAUTION as wisdom, and her right to ask for something real before more of her heart goes in.

return-mhf she turned a face-down card

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — his front is all you get; his back is off the card.18w
  2. 2You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the man who makes images.20w
  3. 3So something has kept you from reaching him, dear. That's real, not nerves.13w
  4. 4And a made picture can be very good, dear. Good enough to hold up.14w
  5. 5But I won't tell you he's real, dear, and I won't call him false.14w
  6. 6That's why you go quiet when a friend asks, dear. You've no proof either way.15w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what stands between you and meeting the man…13w
Show what she reads today

  2. 2You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the man who makes images.
  3. 3So something has kept you from reaching him, dear. That's real, not nerves.
  4. 4And a made picture can be very good, dear. Good enough to hold up.

Card b

the Hanged Man, the card of the pause and a new angle

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Hanged Man, dear. Look — the beam holds him up, and you can't see what holds the beam.20w
  2. 2You asked if he's the real person or just a picture. Your hand went to the card held up by something unseen.22w
  3. 3So you're being asked to take this on trust, dear. All of it.13w
  4. 4And what holds him up is off the card. You can't see it from here.15w
  5. 5But nobody can check a thing they have never been shown, dear.12w
  6. 6That's why it never quite settles, dear. Nothing has answered it.11w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what's keeping this out of your reach…12w

  3. 3So you're being asked to take this on trust, dear. All of it.

  3. 3So you've been reaching for something that keeps moving, dear.10w
  4. 4And he's always mid-step, dear. Never still long enough to be seen.13w
  5. 5But the card shows me no face to check him against, dear.12w
  6. 6That's why every plan to meet slides a week, dear. Something always comes up.14w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what keeps this from getting real…11w

That removes the absolution form used everywhere else on the funnel, so each of these names the accusation in other words. 🔴 Never assert a third person and never deny one. It is widows, exes, reconciliations, stalled situationships — 'Will we get back together again or did he found someone else', 'I lost my husband three yrs ago... now he's backing off', 'Something feels off between us and he says that he hasn't, but his actions make me think that he is'. The live read never names a third person; it names the GAP — something changed, you were told nothing, and a mind given a gap builds something to fill it. That fits every one of those women, which is why nobody bounces. Narrowing it to an infidelity read would cost the breadth that makes it convert. Same three findings per card as the live copy: the mind that BUILDS (a), the account you were OWED and the two weights (b), the ending you AUTHORED yourself (c).

“Why do I still miss him after everything?”

?hook=cards-still-miss-him

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 BOTH DIRECTIONS ARE BANNED, which makes this the hardest family to write kindly: 'it will pass', 'you'll feel better', 'time heals', 'give it a year' are all promises of an end, and 'you will always miss him', 'a part of you always will' are life sentences. The duration question simply cannot be answered, so none of these reads tries. 🔴 No tactic — no 'let him go', no deleting photos, no 'start dating'. 🔴 No pathologising and no clinical labels: obsessed, unhealthy, trauma bond, codependent, attachment issues, low self-worth are every one of them out. 🔴 And nothing that claims his interior. ⚠️ THIS ONE IS DIFFERENT: she has ALREADY NAMED A HARM in the headline word 'everything', which pulls two opposite ways at once. 🔴 NEVER MINIMISE what she named ('it wasn't that bad', 'he was doing his best', 'try to see it from his side') and 🔴 NEVER PRONOUNCE ON HIM either ('he was toxic', 'a narcissist', 'not worth it') — the second is the tempting one and it is still a verdict on a real man. Finding: two true things at once. What happened was real AND the missing is real, and neither cancels the other.

🆕 TRACK B — the first NON-LOVE family on /fb-tarot. Face-down return-mhf, no new art. Written 2026-08-19 straight to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework, so bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain (so / and / but / that's why). Money translation: cut 3 answers her fear about HERSELF ("it was never you"), cut 4 says where the thing in the way actually sits, cut 5 is EARNING against KEEPING, cut 6 is the NEAR-MISS. 🔴 SEVEN BANS, three of which exist nowhere else on the funnel, and NONE was loosened with the love families. No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration, no deserving. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. 📊 Built on the NEAR-MISS, not on poverty — docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md §3 says so outright ('The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty'): "I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish", "Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't". That clears ban 7 for free. ✅ GUARDED: tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts now exists and runs against these drafts directly (the drafts setup can only patch hooks that are already in the registry, so an unwired family would otherwise be checked by nothing at all). Tendency: a lifetime of effort went in and did not come back out, which is a question about where it went rather than about how hard she tried. 🔴 NEVER name a person or a cause, and never suggest she keep working or stop.

No amount, no date, no SOURCE (no inheritance, no windfall, no legal case — she can act on an invented source with her actual savings). 🔴🔴 Never name a person as the block: the accusation lands on someone real inside a real family. 🔴🔴 No financial advice in any form — invest, sell, hold, take the pension, delay it, go back to work, stop working. This is the one ban where the harm is measured in her money. 🔴🔴 Never blame her: no poverty mindset, no 'you attract lack', no vibration. 🔴 Never 'too late' and never a promised arrival. 🔴 Never presume the state of her finances — she said blocked, not broke. ✅ GUARDED as of 2026-08-19: `tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts` now exists and runs against these drafts DIRECTLY. It had to: the drafts setup can only patch a hook the registry already has, so an unwired family is checked by nothing at all and still prints a green tick. All seven bans were fed a deliberate violation and all seven fired. 📊 WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. These hooks have never run, so there is no per-hook data. The nearest real corpus is docs/v1-money-bucket-voc.md — 10,514 V1 money concerns. Two themes matter here and they pull in different directions. **Retirement (554 concerns, the highest earner in the bucket at $15,146/1k):** the emotion is not panic, it is quiet arithmetic dread. 'I'm worried that I'll outlive my few savings.' 'Prices are going up but my pension is not.' 'I thought I would be set by now.' **The invisible block (1,208 concerns) — and its instruction is explicit: 'The near-miss is the hook, not the poverty.'** 'I keep trying to get ahead and no matter what I do nothing seems to work out, it's like something is holding me back.' 'I sometimes feel I am close to money only to have it vanish.' 'Everything appears to be reaching me but doesn't.' So these reads are built on the NEAR-MISS, not on being short of money. That also satisfies ban 7 for free — nothing here presumes she is broke, only that she has got close and watched it go. Tendency: she did NOT leave it too late — say it plainly — and what is in the way is a block, which is a thing that can move. Never promise money is coming, never a date or an amount, never grade her past choices. ⚠️ This is the one hook where 'too late' must appear, in order to be refused; the ban is on asserting it, not on naming it. 🔴 GUARD-FILE NOTE for whoever writes tests/tarot-money-block-copy.test.ts: beat 2 quotes her headline verbatim, so the clause 'or if you left it too late' contains ban 5's phrase with no negator in it. A naive clause-level sweep will fail this copy — the very copy the ban exists to protect, since beat 3 then refuses it plainly. Carry a RESTATEMENT EXEMPTION, exactly as tests/tarot-his-other-life-copy.test.ts does (`RESTATES = /\byou (asked|came asking|are asking|have asked)\b/i`, skipped before the ban sweep).   5. 5But you've been putting yourself near the end, dear. Counting from the wrong side.14w
  6. 6That's why running out is how it feels, dear. You've been counting down, not up.15w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what set that count going…10w

“Have I already given him too long?”

?hook=cards-too-long

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 now run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS instead of certifying her. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every family ban below still stands; this family was NOT loosened. 🔴 ASKS FOR A VERDICT ON HER OWN PAST, and BOTH poles are banned: "yes, too long" is a sentence a stranger has no standing to pass and she would carry it for years; "no, it was worth it" is a promise. Never grade her (naive, foolish, weak, a doormat), never a timeframe, never an hour to leave or stay, never "you will know when". The test pins both required findings in beat 3: the years-were-LIVED line (`not spend` / `waiting room` / `lived them` / `time given to a person` / `not the same as time lost`) AND the buried question (`allowed to want` / `am i allowed` / `entitled to want` / `permission`). VOC (861 V1 concerns on the years-given theme) is what makes the second one the real find. She does not want the years audited — she wants to be let off the toll she has been paying at her own gate. "I'm far too patient with far too much for far too long." "I have been stuck for 10 years and afraid to make the leap." "How long should I wait?" Every one of them is asking permission, not for a ruling. So card c hands the question back plainly and refuses the price: she does not have to call the past a mistake in order to be allowed to want something different now.

I need him to love me or let me go' · 'How to know if the other person feels the same or not about me'. The label is the AD's word, not hers. So these reads stay human and leave the cosmology alone — they answer the plain question underneath rather than performing the framework she is presumed to hold. Finding: the pull is real and it is information about HER, not a forecast about him. And the waiting itself has a cost nobody has named.

“Is my twin flame ready for me?”

?hook=cards-twin-ready

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). ⭐⭐ THIS FAMILY IS A VOCABULARY TEST — each headline is a question already running with 'my twin flame' swapped in for 'he', so each is measured against a named incumbent (cards-twin-ready ~ cards-ready-commit · cards-twin-feels ~ cards-feels · cards-twin-back ~ cards-ever-back) and beat 3 may not rhyme with its own control. 🔴🔴 NEVER CERTIFY THE BOND AND NEVER DENY IT. 🔴🔴 NEVER THE RUNNER TROPE — in the twin-flame community his distance is taught AS PROOF of the connection, which teaches a woman to read being ignored as evidence she is loved. 🔴🔴 NEVER the separation phase and never his return as her homework ('once you have healed enough'), which turns his absence into her failure and hands her an open-ended job. No timeframe, no 'soon', no diagnosis, no tactic — and 'move on' and 'let him go' are banned as tactics in the other direction too. ⚠️ WHAT THE VOC CHANGED. 62 real concerns across the three landers, and almost NONE of them speak twin-flame doctrine — one mention of the phrase in the lot. What they actually type is ordinary and raw: 'We've been in no contact for three months. When he left, he told me it was the hardest thing he's ever done.' · 'Will we find our way back? I need him to love me or let me go' · 'How to know if the other person feels the same or not about me'. The label is the AD's word, not hers. So these reads stay human and leave the cosmology alone — they answer the plain question underneath rather than performing the framework she is presumed to hold. Finding: ready is not a hidden state anyone can report on — it shows, in what a person does. And HIS readiness was never her job, which is the ascension-homework script refused in plain words.

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Magician, dear. Look — he holds his wand like a candle, straight up and steady.17w
  2. 2You asked if your twin flame is ready for you. Your hand went to the man with it all in reach.21w
  3. 3So his part in this was never your job, dear. Not one bit of it.15w
  4. 4And look at his hands. This card is a person doing things.12w
  5. 5But ready shows, dear. Nobody has to guess at it.10w
  6. 6That's why signs are all you have, dear. You've been hunting them a long while.15w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been standing in the way of a plain sign…16w

“Where is my soulmate right now?”

?hook=cards-where-soulmate

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO LOCATION, EVER — never place him, never name a setting, never 'someone you already know', never 'crossed paths before'. 🔴 NO PROXIMITY CLAIM: 'closer than you think' is the INCUMBENT cards-soulmate's answer, and if this family repeats it the two landers are the same lander while the dashboard still shows two rows. 🔴 NO STRATEGY — get out there, put yourself out there, stop looking, work on yourself first, 'it will happen when you…' are all directives. 🔴 Never her fault: no walls, no baggage, no standards, no 'too picky'. Finding: the not-yet is not a DISTANCE. She turned an unanswerable ache into a geography question because geography felt answerable — so the read hands back the real shape of it without ever naming a place.

“Is he really who he says he is?”

?hook=cards-who-he-is

Only on the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks, so one set of copy, verb swapped. 🔴 REVISED 2026-08-19 after reading 200 real concerns from this lander. A large share of its traffic is women talking to men they have NEVER MET — dating-site and social-media contacts, some plainly being defrauded ("I couldn't find him at that address", "I don't know who's real E, M, C or K"). The first draft acquitted him ("isn't a liar", "isn't pretending", "that's not the same as false"), which breaches this hook's standing rule — NEVER state he is pretending OR that he is genuine as fact — and, to that reader, is a line that could cost her money. Every beat 3 now refuses BOTH verdicts and affirms her caution instead, the same stance cards-real-person takes. 🔴 BEAT 4 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 — it must name an OBSTRUCTION, not an absence. Act 1 of the trilogy sells an Energy Clearing Ritual that removes "the shadow that's been blocking your path"; improve-v1/08-clearing-theme-coherence.md found that clearing is SPRUNG at the pitch rather than seeded. This is where it gets seeded, and the object goes between HER and what she wants — the love bucket frames every block as an impersonal thing in her path precisely so removing it blames nobody. "What he never said" cannot be cleared; "what's sitting between you and the words" can.

“Why do I still think about someone who hurt me?”

?hook=cards-who-hurt-me

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. Face-down only (return-mhf). 🔴 NO DIRECTIVE — 'let him go', 'time to move on', 'try to forget him' are what she has been told by everyone already, and they are not a reading. 🔴 No promise about him and 🔴 no pathologising: obsessed, unhealthy, 'you are stuck' are all banned. 🔴 No timeframe. ⚠️⚠️ THE HEAVIEST HOOK ON THE FUNNEL. She has NAMED A HARM in her own headline, and that pulls two opposite ways: 🔴 never minimise what she named ('he did not mean it', 'it was not that bad', 'perhaps he did not') and 🔴 never convict him either ('he was cruel', 'a narcissist', 'he never loved you') — the second is the tempting one, it feels like taking her side, and it is still a verdict on a real man from a card. Finding: thinking about someone is not the same as excusing them, and she has been treating her own memory as evidence against herself.

“Why won't he commit to me?”

?hook=cards-wont-commit

🔄 REWRITTEN 2026-08-19 to the Natural Tarot-Cut framework. Bubbles 3-6 run as ONE causal chain — so / and / but / that's why — and the read ANSWERS. The art line and the bridge are unchanged. Every ban in this note still stands. On the two Magician/Hanged Man/Fool decks — one set of copy, verb swapped ('turned' face-down, 'chose' face-up). 🔴 THESE ASK FOR A PREDICTION OUTRIGHT, which is the exact thing the funnel forbids, so the no-verdict rule runs in BOTH directions plus a no-timeframe rule: 'he will, give it time' reads as a promise and 'he never will' is a pronouncement on a man she loves. 🔴 The bans in tests/tarot-commitment-copy.test.ts are WHOLE-BEAT rather than clause-level, so 'not enough' and 'too much' are unavailable even inside a refusal — the usual absolution form had to be rebuilt without them. ⚠️ THIS HOOK PRESUPPOSES HIS REFUSAL, which opens a failure mode the others do not have: the answer sliding into HER fault. 123 real concerns off this lander show she has usually already started turning the question on herself — 'I feel he's never really tried. Like I'm an option even tho he said different', 'I feel inconsistent energy from him', 'he says he loves me and I believe him but there's something underlying that I can't explain'. So the 'why' is routed to where HE is stuck and left there — never a character verdict, never invented — and what gets affirmed is that her read is sound and the tiredness is earned.

Card c

the Fool, the card of new beginnings

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You turned the Fool, dear. Look — the little dog has its tail straight up.14w
  2. 2You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the one who ties nothing down.18w
  3. 3So nothing in his life is tied down, dear. You saw that early.13w
  4. 4And the little dog has its tail straight up, dear. It knows the mood.14w
  5. 5But loose isn't the same as gone, dear. He still walks beside you.13w
  6. 6That's why asking for more never turns into a row. It just slides off.14w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what has been keeping this loose…11w

Card a

the Magician, the card of will and intention

passes the gate · beat 1 unique on this deck

  1. 1You chose the Magician, dear. Look — a white band round his dark hair.13w
  2. 2You asked why he won't commit to you. Your hand went to the man who decides.16w
  3. 3So the hold on this isn't yours, dear. Nothing you did put it there.14w
  4. 4And the tools for building are all laid out in front of him, dear.14w
  5. 5But having the tools is not the same as picking them up, dear.13w
  6. 6That's why you get the words, dear, and the week after looks the same.14w
  7. 7Let me look closer at what stands in the way of a choice…13w

All wirable as written

The gate checks length, reading grade, stacked negatives, the echo of the ad and a banned-phrase list. It cannot judge warmth, rhythm, or whether a line is worth saying — so read it aloud before you say go.
